[?1049h[24;1H[?1h=UNIQ(1)                     General Commands Manual                    UNIQ(1)

[1mNAME[0m
     [1muniq[0m – report or filter out repeated lines in a file

[1mSYNOPSIS[0m
     [1muniq[0m [[1m-cdiu[0m] [[1m-D[0m[[4mseptype[24m]] [[1m-f[0m [4mnum[24m] [[1m-s[0m [4mchars[24m] [[4minput_file[24m [[4moutput_file[24m]]

[1mDESCRIPTION[0m
     The [1muniq[0m utility reads the specified [4minput_file[24m comparing adjacent lines,
     and writes a copy of each unique input line to the [4moutput_file[24m.  If
     [4minput_file[24m is a single dash (‘[1m-[0m’) or absent, the standard input is read.
     If [4moutput_file[24m is absent, standard output is used for output.  The second
     and succeeding copies of identical adjacent input lines are not written.
     Repeated lines in the input will not be detected if they are not
     adjacent, so it may be necessary to sort the files first.

     The following options are available:

     [1m-c[0m, [1m--count[0m
             Precede each output line with the count of the number of times
             the line occurred in the input, followed by a single space.

:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-d[0m, [1m--repeated[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Output a single copy of each line that is repeated in the input.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Ignored if [1m-D[0m is also specified.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-D[0m, [1m--all-repeated[0m [[4mseptype[24m]
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Output all lines that are repeated (like [1m-d[0m, but each copy of the
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             repeated line is written).  The optional [4mseptype[24m argument
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             controls how to separate groups of repeated lines in the output;
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             it must be one of the following values:
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             none      Do not separate groups of lines (this is the default).
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             prepend   Output an empty line before each group of lines.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             separate  Output an empty line after each group of lines.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-f[0m [4mnum[24m, [1m--skip-fields[0m [4mnum[24m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Ignore the first [4mnum[24m fields in each input line when doing
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             comparisons.  A field is a string of non-blank characters
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             separated from adjacent fields by blanks.  Field numbers are one
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             based, i.e., the first field is field one.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-i[0m, [1m--ignore-case[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Case insensitive comparison of lines.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-s[0m [4mchars[24m, [1m--skip-chars[0m [4mchars[24m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Ignore the first [4mchars[24m characters in each input line when doing
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             comparisons.  If specified in conjunction with the [1m-f[0m, [1m--unique[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             option, the first [4mchars[24m characters after the first [4mnum[24m fields
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             will be ignored.  Character numbers are one based, i.e., the
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             first character is character one.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     [1m-u[0m, [1m--unique[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K             Only output lines that are not repeated in the input.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[1mENVIRONMENT[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     The LANG, LC_ALL, LC_COLLATE and LC_CTYPE environment variables affect
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     the execution of [1muniq[0m as described in environ(7).
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[1mEXIT[0m [1mSTATUS[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     The [1muniq[0m utility exits 0 on success, and >0 if an error occurs.
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K[1mEXAMPLES[0m
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     Assuming a file named cities.txt with the following content:
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Madrid
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Lisbon
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Madrid
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     The following command reports three different lines since identical
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     elements are not adjacent:
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           $ uniq -u cities.txt
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Madrid
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Lisbon
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           Madrid
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     Sort the file and count the number of identical lines:
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           $ sort cities.txt | uniq -c
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K                   1 Lisbon
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K                   2 Madrid
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K     Assuming the following content for the file cities.txt:
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K
:[K[K [KESCESC[KOO[KBB[K           madrid
